main.py
This is the entry point for the application.

Python
import os
import sys
from ai.assistant import Assistant
from hacking_tools.ip_scanner import IPScanner
from game_modding.roblox_modder import RobloxModder
from ui.main_ui import MainUI
from ui.boot_sequence import BootSequence


def initialize_ghostx():
    """
    Initialize the GhostX system and its components.
    """
    print("Initializing GhostX...")

    # Initialize modules
    assistant = Assistant()
    ip_scanner = IPScanner()
    roblox_modder = RobloxModder()
    ui = MainUI()

    print("GhostX Initialized!")

    return {
        "assistant": assistant,
        "ip_scanner": ip_scanner,
        "roblox_modder": roblox_modder,
        "ui": ui,
    }


def main():
    """
    Main entry point for GhostX.
    """
    # Display the boot-up sequence
    boot = BootSequence()
    boot.display_boot_sequence()

    # Initialize GhostX system
    ghostx = initialize_ghostx()

    print("Type 'help' for a list of commands.")

    while True:
        try:
            user_input = input("GhostX> ").strip().lower()
            handle_command(user_input, ghostx)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print("\nExiting GhostX. Goodbye!")
            break


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
ai/assistant.py
Handles AI Assistant functionalities with speech and personality.

Python
import pyttsx3
import speech_recognition as sr
import openai

class Assistant:
    def __init__(self):
        # Initialize text-to-speech engine
        self.tts_engine = pyttsx3.init()
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('rate', 150)
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('volume', 1.0)
        voices = self.tts_engine.getProperty('voices')
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('voice', voices[1].id)

        # Set OpenAI API key
        openai.api_key = "YOUR_OPENAI_API_KEY"

        # Personality settings
        self.personality = "friendly and humorous"

    def speak(self, text):
        self.tts_engine.say(text)
        self.tts_engine.runAndWait()

    def listen(self):
        recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
        with sr.Microphone() as source:
            print("Listening...")
            self.speak("I'm listening. Go ahead!")
            try:
                audio = recognizer.listen(source, timeout=5)
                query = recognizer.recognize_google(audio)
                print(f"You said: {query}")
                return query
            except sr.UnknownValueError:
                self.speak("Sorry, I didn't catch that. Can you repeat?")
                return None
            except sr.RequestError:
                self.speak("Speech recognition service is unavailable.")
                return None
            except Exception as e:
                self.speak(f"An error occurred: {str(e)}")
                return None

    def generate_response(self, query):
        try:
            response = openai.Completion.create(
                engine="text-davinci-003",
                prompt=f"You are a {self.personality} AI assistant. Have a conversation based on this query: {query}",
                max_tokens=150,
                temperature=0.7,
            )
            message = response.choices[0].text.strip()
            return message
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error generating response: {e}")
            return "I'm sorry, but I couldn't process that."

    def chat(self):
        self.speak("Hi! I'm GhostX AI. How can I help you today?")
        while True:
            query = self.listen()
            if query:
                if "exit" in query.lower() or "goodbye" in query.lower():
                    self.speak("Goodbye! Have a great day!")
                    break
                response = self.generate_response(query)
                print(f"Assistant: {response}")
                self.speak(response)
ui/main_ui.py
The main graphical user interface.

Python
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
from ai.assistant import Assistant
import threading


class MainUI:
    def __init__(self):
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        self.root.title("GhostX")
        self.root.geometry("800x600")
        self.assistant = Assistant()
        self.create_widgets()

    def create_widgets(self):
        tk.Label(self.root, text="GhostX - Dashboard", font=("Arial", 24)).pack(pady=20)
        self.chat_frame = ttk.Frame(self.root)
        self.chat_frame.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=20, pady=10)
        self.chat_log = tk.Text(self.chat_frame, state="disabled", wrap="word", bg="#222", fg="#0f0", font=("Courier", 12))
        self.chat_log.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=5, pady=5)
        self.input_frame = ttk.Frame(self.root)
        self.input_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=10)
        self.input_box = ttk.Entry(self.input_frame, font=("Arial", 14))
        self.input_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=5)
        self.input_box.bind("<Return>", self.handle_text_input)
        self.send_button = ttk.Button(self.input_frame, text="Send", command=self.handle_text_input)
        self.send_button.pack(side="left", padx=5)
        self.mic_button = ttk.Button(self.input_frame, text="🎤", command=self.handle_voice_input)
        self.mic_button.pack(side="left", padx=5)

    def handle_text_input(self, event=None):
        user_input = self.input_box.get().strip()
        if user_input:
            self.update_chat_log(f"You: {user_input}")
            self.input_box.delete(0, tk.END)
            threading.Thread(target=self.process_query, args=(user_input,)).start()

    def handle_voice_input(self):
        threading.Thread(target=self.process_voice_input).start()

    def process_query(self, query):
        response = self.assistant.generate_response(query)
        self.update_chat_log(f"Assistant: {response}")
        self.assistant.speak(response)

    def process_voice_input(self):
        query = self.assistant.listen()
        if query:
            self.update_chat_log(f"You: {query}")
            self.process_query(query)

    def update_chat_log(self, message):
        self.chat_log.config(state="normal")
        self.chat_log.insert("end", f"{message}\n")
        self.chat_log.config(state="disabled")
        self.chat_log.see("end")

    def launch(self):
        self.root.mainloop()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ui = MainUI()
    ui.launch()
ui/boot_sequence.py
The animated boot-up sequence.

Python
import time
import sys
from colorama import init, Fore

init(autoreset=True)


class BootSequence:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = [
            "Initializing GhostX Core Modules...",
            "Loading AI Assistant...",
            "Scanning Network Interfaces...",
            "Launching IP Scanner...",
            "Setting up Game Modding Tools...",
            "Finalizing UI Components...",
            "System Integrity Verified ✅",
            "GhostX Boot-Up Complete!"
        ]

    def animate_text(self, text, delay=0.05):
        for char in text:
            sys.stdout.write(char)
            sys.stdout.flush()
            time.sleep(delay)
        sys.stdout.write("\n")

    def display_boot_sequence(self):
        print(Fore.GREEN + """
  ██████╗  ██████╗  ███████╗████████╗██╗  ██╗
 ██╔═══██╗██╔════╝  ██╔════╝╚══██╔══╝██║  ██║
 ██║   ██║██║  ███╗█████╗     ██║   ███████║
 ██║   ██║██║   ██║██╔══╝     ██║   ██╔══██║
 ╚██████╔╝╚██████╔╝███████╗   ██║   ██║  ██║
  ╚═════╝  ╚═════╝ ╚══════╝   ╚═╝   ╚═╝  ╚═╝
        """)

        print(Fore.LIGHTCYAN_EX + "Welcome to GhostX - The Ultimate AI Toolkit\n")
        time.sleep(1)

        for line in self.lines:
            self.animate_text(Fore.LIGHTGREEN_EX + line)
            time.sleep(0.5)

        print(Fore.LIGHTCYAN_EX + "\nLaunching the Main Interface...\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    boot = BootSequence()
    boot.display_boot_sequence()

import os
import sys
from ai.assistant import Assistant
from hacking_tools.ip_scanner import IPScanner
from game_modding.roblox_modder import RobloxModder
from ui.main_ui import MainUI
from ui.boot_sequence import BootSequence

def initialize_ghostx():
    """
    Initialize the GhostX system and its components.
    """
    print("Initializing GhostX...")

    # Initialize modules
    assistant = Assistant()
    ip_scanner = IPScanner()
    roblox_modder = RobloxModder()
    ui = MainUI()

    print("GhostX Initialized!")

    return {
        "assistant": assistant,
        "ip_scanner": ip_scanner,
        "roblox_modder": roblox_modder,
        "ui": ui,
    }

def main():
    """
    Main entry point for GhostX.
    """
    # Display the boot-up sequence
    boot = BootSequence()
    boot.display_boot_sequence()

    # Initialize GhostX system
    ghostx = initialize_ghostx()

    print("Type 'help' for a list of commands.")

    while True:
        try:
            user_input = input("GhostX> ").strip().lower()
            # Handle user commands here (to be implemented)
            if user_input == "exit":
                print("Exiting GhostX... Goodbye!")
                break
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print("\nExiting GhostX. Goodbye!")
            break

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import pyttsx3
import speech_recognition as sr
import openai

class Assistant:
    def __init__(self):
        # Initialize text-to-speech engine
        self.tts_engine = pyttsx3.init()
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('rate', 150)
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('volume', 1.0)
        voices = self.tts_engine.getProperty('voices')
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('voice', voices[1].id)

        # Set OpenAI API key
        openai.api_key = "YOUR_OPENAI_API_KEY"

        # Personality settings
        self.personality = "friendly and humorous"

    def speak(self, text):
        self.tts_engine.say(text)
        self.tts_engine.runAndWait()

    def listen(self):
        recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
        with sr.Microphone() as source:
            print("Listening...")
            self.speak("I'm listening. Go ahead!")
            try:
                audio = recognizer.listen(source, timeout=5)

                print(f"You said: {query}")
                return query

                return None
            except sr.RequestError:
                self.speak("Speech recognition service is unavailable.")
                return None

                self.speak(f"An error occurred: {str(e)}")
                return None

    def generate_response(self, query):
        try:
            response = openai.Completion.create(
                engine="text-davinci-003",

  File "C:\Users\clown\Downloads\GhostX\hacking_tools.py", line 4
    def start_scan(self):
IndentationError: unexpected indent
ChatGPT said:
The IndentationError: unexpected indent error typically occurs when there is inconsistent indentation (spaces vs. tabs or incorrect number of spaces) in your code.

Steps to Fix the Indentation Error:
Open the hacking_tools.py File:

Open the hacking_tools.py file located in C:\Users\clown\Downloads\GhostX using a text editor (e.g., Notepad++ or Visual Studio Code).

Check the Indentation:

Ensure that all the indents in the file are consistent. Python requires that you use either spaces or tabs, not a mix of both. Typically, 4 spaces per indentation level is the standard convention in Python.

Fix the Indentation:

The method start_scan in your hacking_tools.py should start at the same level of indentation as other methods in the class.

Here's an example of how the indentation should look:

import socket

class IPScanner:
    def start_scan(self):
        """
        Perform a basic IP scan on the local network.
        """
        print("IP Scanner: Starting scan...")
        target = "127.0.0.1"  # Replace with actual target IP or range
        try:
            print(f"Scanning target: {target}")
            for port in range(1, 1025):
                with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
                    s.settimeout(0.5)
                    result = s.connect_ex((target, port))
                    if result == 0:
                        print(f"Port {port} is open.")
            print("IP Scanner: Scan complete!")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"An error occurred during the scan: {e}")
Ensure Consistent Indentation:

If you're using spaces, make sure all indentation is done with 4 spaces per indentation level.

If you're using tabs, make sure tabs are used consistently throughout the file.

Avoid mixing tabs and spaces.

        Perform a basic IP scan on the local network.
        """
        print("IP Scanner: Starting scan...")
        target = "127.0.0.1"  # Replace with actual target IP or range
        try:
            print(f"Scanning target: {target}")
            for port in range(1, 1025):
                with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
                    s.settimeout(0.5)
                    result = s.connect_ex((target, port))
                    if result == 0:
                        print(f"Port {port} is open.")
            print("IP Scanner: Scan complete!")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"An error occurred during the scan: {e}")

    roblox_modder = RobloxModder()

You said:
i need the full main.py code
ChatGPT said:
Certainly! Below is the full main.py code with all the necessary changes. This includes passing the OpenAI API key when initializing the Assistant class and proper handling of the components.

Full main.py Code:
import os
import sys
from ai.assistant import Assistant
from hacking_tools.ip_scanner import IPScanner
from game_modding.roblox_modder import RobloxModder
from ui.main_ui import MainUI
from ui.boot_sequence import BootSequence

def initialize_ghostx():
    """
    Initialize the GhostX system and its components.
    """
    print("Initializing GhostX...")

    # Load the OpenAI API key from environment variable or hardcode it
    api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
    
    if not api_key:
        raise ValueError("API Key is missing. Please set the OPENAI_API_KEY environment variable.")

    # Initialize components
    assistant = Assistant(api_key=api_key)
    ip_scanner = IPScanner()
    roblox_modder = RobloxModder()
    ui = MainUI()

    print("GhostX Initialized!")

    return {
        "assistant": assistant,
        "ip_scanner": ip_scanner,
        "roblox_modder": roblox_modder,
        "ui": ui,
    }

def handle_command(user_input, ghostx):
    """
    Handle user commands and execute corresponding actions.
    """
    if user_input == "help":
        print("Available commands: help, scan, mod, exit")
    elif user_input == "scan":
        print("Launching IP Scanner...")
        ghostx["ip_scanner"].start_scan()
    elif user_input == "mod":
        print("Launching Roblox Modding Tool...")
        ghostx["roblox_modder"].mod_game()
    elif user_input == "exit":
        print("Exiting GhostX... Goodbye!")
        sys.exit(0)
    else:
        print(f"Unknown command: {user_input}. Type 'help' for a list of commands.")

def main():
    """
    Main entry point for GhostX.
    """
    # Display the boot-up sequence
    boot = BootSequence()
    boot.display_boot_sequence()

    # Initialize GhostX system
    ghostx = initialize_ghostx()

    print("Type 'help' for a list of commands.")

    while True:
        try:
            user_input = input("GhostX> ").strip().lower()
            handle_command(user_input, ghostx)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print("\nExiting GhostX. Goodbye!")
            break

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
Key Updates and Explanations:
Loading the OpenAI API Key:

The script first tries to get the API key from an environment variable (OPENAI_API_KEY).

If the environment variable is not set, it raises an error with the message: "API Key is missing. Please set the OPENAI_API_KEY environment variable.".

Error Handling for Missing API Key:

If you haven't set the OPENAI_API_KEY environment variable, the program will raise a ValueError and exit gracefully.

Functionality for Commands:

The handle_command() function processes user input like help, scan, mod, and exit.

For example, if the user types scan, it runs the IP scanner. If the user types mod, it launches the Roblox modding tool.

Boot Sequence:

The BootSequence class displays a boot-up sequence on the terminal, simulating the loading process.

import openai

class Assistant:
    def __init__(self, api_key):
        """
        Initialize the Assistant with the provided OpenAI API key.
        """
        openai.api_key = api_key

    def get_response(self, prompt: str) -> str:
        """
        Get a response from the AI assistant based on the provided prompt.
        
        :param prompt: The user input or query.
        :return: AI-generated response as a string.
        """
        try:
            response = openai.Completion.create(
                engine="text-davinci-003",  # You can change this if needed
                prompt=prompt,
                max_tokens=150,
                n=1,
                stop=None,
                temperature=0.7,
            )
            return response.choices[0].text.strip()
        except Exception as e:
            return f"Error: {e}"

def initialize_ghostx(api_key):
    """
    Initialize the GhostX system and its components.
    """

    assistant = Assistant(api_key=api_key)
    ip_scanner = IPScanner()

ChatGPT said:
The error TypeError: Assistant.__init__() missing 1 required positional argument: 'api_key' occurs because the Assistant class requires the api_key to be passed when it's initialized, but in ui/main_ui.py, it's being instantiated without the api_key.

To resolve this, follow these steps:

Updated main_ui.py:
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk
from ai.assistant import Assistant  # Make sure this import is correct

class MainUI:
    def __init__(self, api_key):
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        self.root.title("GhostX")
        self.root.geometry("900x700")
        
        # Pass the api_key when initializing Assistant
        self.assistant = Assistant(api_key)  # Fix: pass api_key here
        
        self.theme = "dark"  # Default theme
        self.create_widgets()

    def create_widgets(self):
        # Menu bar
        self.create_menu()

        # Notebook (tabs)
        self.notebook = ttk.Notebook(self.root)
        self.notebook.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=10, pady=10)

        # Tabs
        self.create_dashboard_tab()
        self.create_logs_tab()

    def create_menu(self):
        menu_bar = tk.Menu(self.root)
        self.root.config(menu=menu_bar)

        settings_menu = tk.Menu(menu_bar, tearoff=0)
        settings_menu.add_command(label="Switch to Light Mode", command=self.switch_theme)
        menu_bar.add_cascade(label="Settings", menu=settings_menu)

    def create_dashboard_tab(self):
        dashboard_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(dashboard_tab, text="Dashboard")

        # Chat log
        self.chat_log = tk.Text(dashboard_tab, state="disabled", wrap="word", bg="#222", fg="#0f0", font=("Courier", 12))
        self.chat_log.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=10, pady=10)

        # Input frame
        input_frame = ttk.Frame(dashboard_tab)
        input_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=10)

        self.input_box = ttk.Entry(input_frame, font=("Arial", 14))
        self.input_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=5)
        self.input_box.bind("<Return>", self.handle_text_input)

        self.send_button = ttk.Button(input_frame, text="Send", command=self.handle_text_input)
        self.send_button.pack(side="left", padx=5)

        self.mic_button = ttk.Button(input_frame, text="🎤 Listen", command=self.handle_voice_input)
        self.mic_button.pack(side="left", padx=5)

    def create_logs_tab(self):
        logs_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(logs_tab, text="Logs")

        # Logs text box
        self.logs_text = tk.Text(logs_tab, state="disabled", wrap="word", bg="#111", fg="#fff", font=("Courier", 12))
        self.logs_text.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=10, pady=10)

    def switch_theme(self):
        if self.theme == "dark":
            self.theme = "light"
            self.root.tk_setPalette(background="white", foreground="black")
        else:
            self.theme = "dark"
            self.root.tk_setPalette(background="black", foreground="white")

    def handle_text_input(self, event=None):
        user_input = self.input_box.get().strip()
        if user_input:
            self.update_chat_log(f"You: {user_input}")
            self.input_box.delete(0, tk.END)
            self.process_query(user_input)

    def handle_voice_input(self):
        self.process_voice_input()

    def process_query(self, query):
        response = self.assistant.get_response(query)
        self.update_chat_log(f"Assistant: {response}")
        self.assistant.speak(response)

    def process_voice_input(self):
        query = self.assistant.listen()
        if query:
            self.update_chat_log(f"You: {query}")
            self.process_query(query)

    def update_chat_log(self, message):
        self.chat_log.config(state="normal")
        self.chat_log.insert("end", f"{message}\n")
        self.chat_log.config(state="disabled")
        self.chat_log.see("end")

    def launch(self):
        self.root.mainloop()

from tkwebview2.tkwebview2 import WebView2  # For in-app browser
import vlc                                  # For in-app movie player
✅ Then in MainUI.__init__(), after self.create_logs_tab(), add:
self.create_movie_tab()
self.create_web_tab()
✅ Then at the bottom of the class, add:
def create_movie_tab(self):
    movie_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
    self.notebook.add(movie_tab, text="🎬 Movies")

    label = ttk.Label(movie_tab, text="Paste a video URL (MP4, stream, etc.):")
    label.pack(pady=10)

    self.video_url = ttk.Entry(movie_tab, font=("Arial", 12), width=60)
    self.video_url.pack(pady=5)

    play_button = ttk.Button(movie_tab, text="▶ Play", command=self.play_movie)
    play_button.pack(pady=5)

    self.movie_frame = tk.Frame(movie_tab, bg="black", width=800, height=450)
    self.movie_frame.pack(padx=10, pady=10)

def play_movie(self):
    url = self.video_url.get()
    if not url:
        return
    instance = vlc.Instance()
    self.player = instance.media_player_new()
    self.player.set_mrl(url)
    self.player.set_hwnd(self.movie_frame.winfo_id())
    self.player.play()

def create_web_tab(self):
    web_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
    self.notebook.add(web_tab, text="🌐 Web")

    self.browser = WebView2(web_tab, width=800, height=600)
    self.browser.pack(expand=True, fill="both")
    self.browser.load_url("https://duckduckgo.com")  # Or your stealth search engine

📂 File: ui/main_ui.py
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
from ai.assistant import Assistant
from tkwebview2.tkwebview2 import WebView2
import vlc
import threading


class MainUI:
    def __init__(self, api_key):
        self.api_key = api_key
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        self.root.title("GhostX")
        self.root.geometry("1000x700")
        self.assistant = Assistant(api_key=self.api_key)
        self.theme = "dark"
        self.create_widgets()

    def create_widgets(self):
        self.create_menu()
        self.notebook = ttk.Notebook(self.root)
        self.notebook.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=10, pady=10)
        self.create_dashboard_tab()
        self.create_logs_tab()
        self.create_movie_tab()
        self.create_web_tab()

    def create_menu(self):
        menu_bar = tk.Menu(self.root)
        self.root.config(menu=menu_bar)
        settings_menu = tk.Menu(menu_bar, tearoff=0)
        settings_menu.add_command(label="Switch Theme", command=self.switch_theme)
        menu_bar.add_cascade(label="Settings", menu=settings_menu)

    def switch_theme(self):
        self.theme = "light" if self.theme == "dark" else "dark"
        bg = "white" if self.theme == "light" else "black"
        fg = "black" if self.theme == "light" else "lime"
        self.chat_log.config(bg=bg, fg=fg)

    def create_dashboard_tab(self):
        dashboard_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(dashboard_tab, text="🧠 Assistant")

        self.chat_log = tk.Text(dashboard_tab, state="disabled", wrap="word", bg="black", fg="lime", font=("Courier", 12))
        self.chat_log.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=10, pady=10)

        input_frame = ttk.Frame(dashboard_tab)
        input_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=10)

        self.input_box = ttk.Entry(input_frame, font=("Arial", 14))
        self.input_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=5)
        self.input_box.bind("<Return>", self.handle_text_input)

        self.send_button = ttk.Button(input_frame, text="Send", command=self.handle_text_input)
        self.send_button.pack(side="left", padx=5)

        self.mic_button = ttk.Button(input_frame, text="🎤", command=self.handle_voice_input)
        self.mic_button.pack(side="left", padx=5)

    def handle_text_input(self, event=None):
        user_input = self.input_box.get().strip()
        if user_input:
            self.update_chat_log(f"You: {user_input}")
            self.input_box.delete(0, tk.END)
            threading.Thread(target=self.process_query, args=(user_input,)).start()

    def handle_voice_input(self):
        threading.Thread(target=self.process_voice_input).start()

    def process_query(self, query):
        response = self.assistant.get_response(query)
        self.update_chat_log(f"Ghost: {response}")
        self.assistant.speak(response)

        # Command Parsing (experimental)
        if query.lower().startswith("search"):
            search_term = query.replace("search", "").strip()
            self.browser.load_url(f"https://duckduckgo.com/?q={search_term}")
            self.notebook.select(self.web_tab)
        elif query.lower().startswith("play movie"):
            link = query.replace("play movie", "").strip()
            self.video_url.delete(0, tk.END)
            self.video_url.insert(0, link)
            self.play_movie()
            self.notebook.select(self.movie_tab)

    def process_voice_input(self):
        query = self.assistant.listen()
        if query:
            self.update_chat_log(f"You: {query}")
            self.process_query(query)

    def update_chat_log(self, message):
        self.chat_log.config(state="normal")
        self.chat_log.insert("end", f"{message}\n")
        self.chat_log.config(state="disabled")
        self.chat_log.see("end")

    def create_logs_tab(self):
        logs_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(logs_tab, text="📜 Logs")

        self.logs_text = tk.Text(logs_tab, state="disabled", wrap="word", bg="#111", fg="#fff", font=("Courier", 12))
        self.logs_text.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=10, pady=10)

    def create_movie_tab(self):
        self.movie_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(self.movie_tab, text="🎬 Movies")

        ttk.Label(self.movie_tab, text="Paste video URL (MP4/M3U8):").pack(pady=10)
        self.video_url = ttk.Entry(self.movie_tab, font=("Arial", 12), width=70)
        self.video_url.pack(pady=5)

        play_button = ttk.Button(self.movie_tab, text="▶ Play", command=self.play_movie)
        play_button.pack(pady=5)

        self.movie_frame = tk.Frame(self.movie_tab, bg="black", width=880, height=460)
        self.movie_frame.pack(padx=10, pady=10)

    def play_movie(self):
        url = self.video_url.get()
        if not url:
            messagebox.showwarning("No URL", "Please enter a video URL.")
            return
        instance = vlc.Instance()
        self.player = instance.media_player_new()
        self.player.set_mrl(url)
        self.player.set_hwnd(self.movie_frame.winfo_id())
        self.player.play()

    def create_web_tab(self):
        self.web_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(self.web_tab, text="🌐 Web")

        self.browser = WebView2(self.web_tab, width=980, height=620)
        self.browser.pack(expand=True, fill="both")

        self.browser.load_url("https://duckduckgo.com")  # Privacy-first default search


    def launch(self):
        self.root.mainloop()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    api_key = "your_openai_api_key"
    ui = MainUI(api_key)
    ui.launch()

import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
import threading
import vlc
import os
from tkwebview2.tkwebview2 import WebView2
import pyttsx3
import speech_recognition as sr
import openai


class Assistant:
    def __init__(self, api_key):
        openai.api_key = api_key
        self.tts_engine = pyttsx3.init()
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('rate', 150)
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('volume', 1.0)
        voices = self.tts_engine.getProperty('voices')
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('voice', voices[1].id)

    def speak(self, text):
        self.tts_engine.say(text)
        self.tts_engine.runAndWait()

    def listen(self):
        recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
        with sr.Microphone() as source:
            self.speak("I'm listening.")
            try:
                audio = recognizer.listen(source, timeout=5)
                query = recognizer.recognize_google(audio)
                print(f"Voice input: {query}")
                return query
            except:
                self.speak("I didn't catch that.")
                return None

    def get_response(self, prompt: str) -> str:
        try:
            response = openai.Completion.create(
                engine="text-davinci-003",
                prompt=prompt,
                max_tokens=150,
                temperature=0.7
            )
            return response.choices[0].text.strip()
        except Exception as e:
            return f"Error: {e}"


class GhostXUI:
    def __init__(self, api_key):
        self.api_key = api_key
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        self.root.title("GhostX OS - Undetectable Core")
        self.root.geometry("1080x720")
        self.assistant = Assistant(api_key=self.api_key)
        self.theme = "dark"
        self.create_widgets()

    def create_widgets(self):
        self.create_menu()
        self.notebook = ttk.Notebook(self.root)
        self.notebook.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=10, pady=10)
        self.create_ai_tab()
        self.create_logs_tab()
        self.create_movie_tab()
        self.create_web_tab()
        self.create_placeholder_tab("🎮 Roblox Zone")
        self.create_placeholder_tab("🎯 Fortnite Zone")
        self.create_placeholder_tab("🧱 Minecraft Zone")
        self.create_placeholder_tab("🤖 Bot Master")
        self.create_placeholder_tab("💣 Payloads + Exploits")

    def create_menu(self):
        menu_bar = tk.Menu(self.root)
        self.root.config(menu=menu_bar)
        settings_menu = tk.Menu(menu_bar, tearoff=0)
        settings_menu.add_command(label="Switch Theme", command=self.switch_theme)
        menu_bar.add_cascade(label="Settings", menu=settings_menu)

    def switch_theme(self):
        self.theme = "light" if self.theme == "dark" else "dark"
        bg = "white" if self.theme == "light" else "black"
        fg = "black" if self.theme == "light" else "lime"
        self.chat_log.config(bg=bg, fg=fg)

    def create_ai_tab(self):
        ai_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(ai_tab, text="🧠 Assistant")

        self.chat_log = tk.Text(ai_tab, state="disabled", wrap="word", bg="black", fg="lime", font=("Courier", 12))
        self.chat_log.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=10, pady=10)

        input_frame = ttk.Frame(ai_tab)
        input_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=10)

        self.input_box = ttk.Entry(input_frame, font=("Arial", 14))
        self.input_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=5)
        self.input_box.bind("<Return>", self.handle_text_input)

        self.send_button = ttk.Button(input_frame, text="Send", command=self.handle_text_input)
        self.send_button.pack(side="left", padx=5)

        self.mic_button = ttk.Button(input_frame, text="🎤", command=self.handle_voice_input)
        self.mic_button.pack(side="left", padx=5)

    def handle_text_input(self, event=None):
        user_input = self.input_box.get().strip()
        if user_input:
            self.update_chat_log(f"You: {user_input}")
            self.input_box.delete(0, tk.END)
            threading.Thread(target=self.process_query, args=(user_input,)).start()

    def handle_voice_input(self):
        threading.Thread(target=self.process_voice_input).start()

    def process_query(self, query):
        response = self.assistant.get_response(query)
        self.update_chat_log(f"Ghost: {response}")
        self.assistant.speak(response)

        # Command Parsing
        if query.lower().startswith("search"):
            term = query.replace("search", "").strip()
            self.browser.load_url(f"https://duckduckgo.com/?q={term}")
            self.notebook.select(self.web_tab)
        elif query.lower().startswith("play movie"):
            link = query.replace("play movie", "").strip()
            self.video_url.delete(0, tk.END)
            self.video_url.insert(0, link)
            self.play_movie()
            self.notebook.select(self.movie_tab)

    def process_voice_input(self):
        query = self.assistant.listen()
        if query:
            self.update_chat_log(f"You: {query}")
            self.process_query(query)

    def update_chat_log(self, message):
        self.chat_log.config(state="normal")
        self.chat_log.insert("end", f"{message}\n")
        self.chat_log.config(state="disabled")
        self.chat_log.see("end")

    def create_logs_tab(self):
        logs_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(logs_tab, text="📜 Logs")
        self.logs_text = tk.Text(logs_tab, state="disabled", wrap="word", bg="#111", fg="#fff", font=("Courier", 12))
        self.logs_text.pack(expand=True, fill="both", padx=10, pady=10)

    def create_movie_tab(self):
        self.movie_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(self.movie_tab, text="🎬 Movies")

        ttk.Label(self.movie_tab, text="Paste video URL (MP4/M3U8):").pack(pady=10)
        self.video_url = ttk.Entry(self.movie_tab, font=("Arial", 12), width=70)
        self.video_url.pack(pady=5)

        play_button = ttk.Button(self.movie_tab, text="▶ Play", command=self.play_movie)
        play_button.pack(pady=5)

        self.movie_frame = tk.Frame(self.movie_tab, bg="black", width=880, height=460)
        self.movie_frame.pack(padx=10, pady=10)

    def play_movie(self):
        url = self.video_url.get()
        if not url:
            messagebox.showwarning("No URL", "Please enter a video URL.")
            return
        instance = vlc.Instance()
        self.player = instance.media_player_new()
        self.player.set_mrl(url)
        self.player.set_hwnd(self.movie_frame.winfo_id())
        self.player.play()

    def create_web_tab(self):
        self.web_tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(self.web_tab, text="🌐 Web")
        self.browser = WebView2(self.web_tab, width=980, height=620)
        self.browser.pack(expand=True, fill="both")
        self.browser.load_url("https://duckduckgo.com")

    def create_placeholder_tab(self, title):
        tab = ttk.Frame(self.notebook)
        self.notebook.add(tab, text=title)
        ttk.Label(tab, text=f"{title} is under construction...", font=("Arial", 16)).pack(pady=30)

    def launch(self):
        self.root.mainloop()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    api_key = "your_openai_api_key"  # Replace with your actual key
    app = GhostXUI(api_key)
    app.launch()

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';

// Define types for better state management
type Theme = 'dark' | 'neon' | 'glitch' | 'matrix';
type ActiveTab = 'aiHub' | 'executorZone' | 'hackTools' | 'modLab' | 'webArsenal' | 'botZone' | 'settings' | 'updates';
type VoiceStatus = 'inactive' | 'listening' | 'processing';
type VoicePersonality = 'Ghost' | 'Luna' | 'HackerBot';

// Helper component for Section Placeholders
const SectionPlaceholder: React.FC<{ title: string; children?: React.ReactNode }> = ({ title, children }) => (
  <div className="p-6 bg-gray-800 rounded-lg shadow-lg h-full overflow-y-auto">
    <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">{title}</h2>
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {children || <p className="text-gray-400">Content for {title} goes here...</p>}
    </div>
  </div>
);

// Helper component for interactive buttons
const ActionButton: React.FC<{ text: string; onClick?: () => void; accentColor?: string }> = ({ text, onClick, accentColor = 'cyan' }) => (
  <button
    onClick={onClick}
    className={px-4 py-2 rounded bg-${accentColor}-600 hover:bg-${accentColor}-500 text-white font-semibold transition duration-150 ease-in-out shadow-md}
  >
    {text}
  </button>
);

export default function GhostXApp() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<ActiveTab>('aiHub');
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>('dark');
  const [voiceStatus, setVoiceStatus] = useState<VoiceStatus>('inactive');
  const [voicePersonality, setVoicePersonality] = useState<VoicePersonality>('Ghost');
  const [terminalInput, setTerminalInput] = useState<string>('');
  const [terminalOutput, setTerminalOutput] = useState<string[]>(['GhostX Terminal Initialized. Waiting for commands...']);
  const [showBootAnimation, setShowBootAnimation] = useState<boolean>(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Simulate boot animation duration
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setShowBootAnimation(false), 1500); // Adjust time as needed
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, []);

  const handleTerminalSubmit = useCallback((e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!terminalInput.trim()) return;

    const command = terminalInput.trim();
    const output = > ${command};
    const response = Processing: ${command}... (Simulated); // Simulate command processing

    setTerminalOutput(prev => [...prev, output, response]);
    setTerminalInput('');

    // Simulate voice activation command
    if (command.toLowerCase().startsWith('ghost')) {
      setVoiceStatus('listening');
      setTimeout(() => setVoiceStatus('processing'), 1000);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setTerminalOutput(prev => [...prev, [${voicePersonality}]: Executing command...]);
        setVoiceStatus('inactive');
      }, 2500);
    }
  }, [terminalInput, voicePersonality]);

  // Theme class generation
  const getThemeClasses = () => {
    switch (theme) {
      case 'neon':
        return {
          bg: 'bg-black',
          text: 'text-lime-400',
          accent: 'lime',
          border: 'border-lime-500',
          sidebarBg: 'bg-gray-900',
          sidebarAccent: 'lime',
          terminalText: 'text-lime-300',
          glitchEffect: ''
        };
      case 'glitch':
         return {
          bg: 'bg-gray-900',
          text: 'text-purple-400',
          accent: 'purple',
          border: 'border-purple-600',
          sidebarBg: 'bg-black',
          sidebarAccent: 'purple',
          terminalText: 'text-purple-300',

        };
      case 'matrix':
         return {
          bg: 'bg-black',
          text: 'text-green-500 font-mono', // Matrix font style

          border: 'border-green-600',
          sidebarBg: 'bg-gray-950',
          sidebarAccent: 'green',
          terminalText: 'text-green-400 font-mono',
          glitchEffect: ''
        };
      case 'dark':
      default:
        return {
          bg: 'bg-gray-900',
          text: 'text-gray-200',
          accent: 'cyan',
          border: 'border-gray-700',
          sidebarBg: 'bg-gray-800',
          sidebarAccent: 'cyan',
          terminalText: 'text-cyan-300',
          glitchEffect: ''
        };
    }
  };

  const themeClasses = getThemeClasses();

  if (showBootAnimation) {
    return (
      <div className={flex items-center justify-center min-h-screen ${themeClasses.bg} ${themeClasses.text}}>
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center animate-pulse">
           {/* Glitch Bolt SVG Placeholder - Replace with actual complex SVG/animation */}
           <svg className={w-24 h-24 mb-4 text-${themeClasses.accent}-500} fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path fillRule="evenodd" d="M11.3 1.046A1 1 0 0112 2v5h4a1 1 0 01.82 1.573l-7 10A1 1 0 018 18v-5H4a1 1 0 01-.82-1.573l7-10a1 1 0 011.12-.38z" clipRule="evenodd"></path></svg>
          <p className={text-xl font-bold ${themeClasses.glitchEffect}}>GHOSTX INITIALIZING...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

    { id: 'aiHub', name: 'AI Hub', icon: '🧠' },
    { id: 'executorZone', name: 'Executor Zone', icon: '🎮' },
    { id: 'hackTools', name: 'Hack Tools', icon: '🛠️' },
    { id: 'modLab', name: 'Mod Lab', icon: '🧪' },
    { id: 'webArsenal', name: 'Web Arsenal', icon: '🌐' },
    { id: 'botZone', name: 'Bot Zone', icon: '🤖' },
    { id: 'settings', name: 'Settings', icon: '⚙️' },
    { id: 'updates', name: 'Updates', icon: '🔄' },

                         <ActionButton text="Create Instant Proxy" accentColor={themeClasses.accent} />
                    </div>
                     <div className={p-4 rounded border ${themeClasses.border} bg-gray-800}>

                         <ActionButton text="Start Scan" accentColor={themeClasses.accent} />
                    </div>
                </div>
            </SectionPlaceholder>
          )}
          {activeTab === 'botZone' && (
            <SectionPlaceholder title="Bot Zone">
                <p>Configure and run automated tasks and game bots.</p>
                 <div className={p-4 rounded border ${themeClasses.border} bg-gray-800 mb-4}>

            </SectionPlaceholder>

           {activeTab === 'settings' && (

                    <option value="dark">Dark</option>
                    <option value="neon">Neon</option>
                    <option value="glitch">Glitch</option>

                </div>

                  <select value={voicePersonality} onChange={(e) => setVoicePersonality(e.target.value as VoicePersonality)} className={w-full p-2 rounded ${themeClasses.bg} border ${themeClasses.border} text-gray-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-${themeClasses.accent}-500}>
                    <option value="Ghost">Ghost (Default)</option>
                    <option value="Luna">Luna</option>
                    <option value="HackerBot">HackerBot</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                 <div>

                    <label className="flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer">

                        <span>Enable Process Hiding</span>
                    </label>
                     <label className="flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer mt-2">

                    </label>
                </div>
                 <div>

                     <ActionButton text="Connect Cloud Account" accentColor={themeClasses.accent} />

              </div>
            </SectionPlaceholder>
          )}
            {activeTab === 'updates' && (
            <SectionPlaceholder title="Update Engine">
                <p>GhostX Auto-Evolution System</p>
                 <div className={p-4 rounded border ${themeClasses.border} bg-gray-800 mb-4}>

                     <ActionButton text="Browse Community Plugins" accentColor={themeClasses.accent} />
                     <ActionButton text="Load Local Plugin" accentColor="gray" className="ml-2" />
                </div>
            </SectionPlaceholder>
          )}
        </div>

         {/* Integrated Terminal */}
         <div className={h-48 border-t ${themeClasses.border} p-2 flex flex-col bg-black}>
             <div className={flex-1 overflow-y-auto text-xs ${themeClasses.terminalText} font-mono mb-1 pr-2}>
                 {terminalOutput.map((line, index) => (
                    <div key={index}>{line}</div>
                 ))}
             </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleTerminalSubmit} className="flex items-center">
                <span className={mr-2 text-${themeClasses.accent}-400}>$</span>
                <input
                    type="text"
                    value={terminalInput}
                    onChange={(e) => setTerminalInput(e.target.value)}
                    className={flex-1 bg-transparent border-none focus:outline-none text-sm ${themeClasses.terminalText} font-mono}
                    placeholder="Enter command... (e.g., 'ghost scan 192.168.1.1')"
                />
            </form>
         </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

@echo off
title GHOSTX - SYSTEM INSTALLER

echo Initializing GhostX Deployment...
timeout /t 2 >nul

:: Set target directory
set "GXDIR=%USERPROFILE%\Desktop\GhostX"

:: Create folder structure
mkdir "%GXDIR%"
mkdir "%GXDIR%\ai"
mkdir "%GXDIR%\mods"
mkdir "%GXDIR%\ui"
mkdir "%GXDIR%\botnet"
mkdir "%GXDIR%\movie"
mkdir "%GXDIR%\payloads"
mkdir "%GXDIR%\config"
mkdir "%GXDIR%\sounds"

:: Create placeholder files
echo @echo off > "%GXDIR%\GhostX.exe"
echo Voice AI loading... >> "%GXDIR%\ai\assistant.py"
echo Roblox mod engine active >> "%GXDIR%\mods\roblox_mod.py"
echo Fortnite OP mode enabled >> "%GXDIR%\mods\fortnite_mod.py"
echo Terminal UI booted >> "%GXDIR%\ui\main_ui.py"
echo Bot controller online >> "%GXDIR%\botnet\auto_farm.py"

🧠 AI Assistant (with Voice & OpenAI):
Create this file inside:

GhostX\ai\assistant.py
Paste this inside it:

import pyttsx3
import speech_recognition as sr
import openai

class Assistant:
    def __init__(self, api_key):
        self.api_key = api_key
        openai.api_key = api_key
        self.personality = "You are a glitchy, sarcastic, powerful AI assistant called Ghost."

        self.tts_engine = pyttsx3.init()
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('rate', 160)
        voices = self.tts_engine.getProperty('voices')
        self.tts_engine.setProperty('voice', voices[1].id)

    def speak(self, text):
        self.tts_engine.say(text)
        self.tts_engine.runAndWait()

    def listen(self):
        recognizer = sr.Recognizer()
        with sr.Microphone() as source:
            print("🎙️ Listening...")
            audio = recognizer.listen(source, timeout=5)
            try:
                query = recognizer.recognize_google(audio)
                print(f"You said: {query}")
                return query
            except Exception:
                self.speak("Didn't catch that. Say it again, Commander.")
                return None

    def generate_response(self, query):
        try:
            response = openai.Completion.create(
                engine="text-davinci-003",
